Even picking which spreadsheet to start with can feel… weirdly hard.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period like this before, where you felt this consistently low and fatigued, or this is the first time?\n\nPatient: I’m… not really sure. I’ve had rough patches before, I guess, but I don’t know if they were like this. I’d say the low, exhausted, slowed-down feeling has been there most days for maybe… around two months now.\n\nDoctor: Have you had multiple times in the past when you felt this low, lost interest in things, or had trouble with energy, or has this only happened once before?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I mean, I’ve had times where I wasn’t doing great, but whether it was… the same kind of low, or more than once like this, I’m not sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one separate period in your life when you felt this low, lost interest in things, or had trouble with your energy—where each period lasted for several weeks or more?\n\nPatient: I… I’m not sure. I don’t really keep track of it like that. There have been bad stretches, but I don’t know if they were separate periods lasting several weeks or… just life being hard.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one separate time in your life when you felt this low, lost interest in things, or had trouble with your energy—where each time lasted for several weeks or more?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know. I know there’ve been rough patches, but I can’t really say if they were separate times lasting weeks like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one separate time in your life when you felt this low, lost interest in things, or had trouble with your energy—where each time lasted for several weeks or more, and you had a clear period of improvement in between?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I mean… I can think of times I felt better after bad stretches, but I can’t say clearly whether there were multiple separate periods like that, with a definite improvement in between.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one separate time in your life when you felt this low, lost interest in things, or had trouble with your energy—where each time lasted for several weeks or more, and you had a clear period of improvement in between?\n\nPatient: I’m sorry, I just… I don’t have a clear answer.
